NV_BASIC#
Basic Skill
Max Level: 9
Skill Form: Passive
Description: Enables the use of Basic
Interface Skills.
[Lv 1]: Enables Trading
You can trade Item and Zeny with other
Player.
Right click on a player to initiate.
[Lv 2]: Enables Emotions
Alt+(0~9) and Ctrl+(1,-,=.)
Alt+L will open Emoticon Window.
[Lv 3]: Enables Sitting
Doubles HP and SP regen speed.
Press the Insert key or type /sit.
[Lv 4]: Enables Chat Room Creation
Alt+C to open a Chat Room.
[Lv 5]: Joins a Party
Character can join a party.
[Lv 6]: Enables Use of Kafra Storage
Talk to Kafra Staff to open Storage.
[Lv 7]: Organize a Party
Creates a party
Type /organize "Party Name"
Set party options in the Party Window (Alt+Z).
[Lv 9]: Enables Change to First Job Class
Qualifies character for change from
Novice to one of the First or Expanded
classes.

SM_RECOVERY#
Increase HP Recovery
Max Level: 10
Skill Form: Passive
Description: Restores HP every
10 seconds while idle. MaxHP affects how much
HP is restored by this skill.
[Lv 1]: Restores (5 + 0.2% of MaxHP) HP
[Lv 2]: Restores (10 + 0.4% of MaxHP) HP
[Lv 3]: Restores (15 + 0.6% of MaxHP) HP
[Lv 4]: Restores (20 + 0.8% of MaxHP) HP
[Lv 5]: Restores (25 + 1.0% of MaxHP) HP
[Lv 6]: Restores (30 + 1.2% of MaxHP) HP
[Lv 7]: Restores (35 + 1.4% of MaxHP) HP
[Lv 8]: Restores (40 + 1.6% of MaxHP) HP
[Lv 9]: Restores (45 + 1.8% of MaxHP) HP
[Lv 10]: Restores (50 + 2.0% of MaxHP) HP

#
AL_WARP#
Warp Portal
Max Level: 4
Requirement: Teleport 2
Skill Form: Active
Type: Supportive
Target: Ground
Description: Creates a Warp Portal that
will teleport those who enter it to
another area. Each Warp Portal can
teleport up to 8 people. Can't be used
in an area that protected by Land
Protector.
Consumes 1 Blue Gemstone.
[Lv 1]: Back to caster's Save Point
[Lv 2]: Enables 1 Memo point
[Lv 3]: Enables 2 Memo points
[Lv 4]: Enables 3 Memo points
[Memo Point]: Specific map area
memorized as a destination for a Warp
Portal.
Type/memo to set current location
as a Memo Point.

Lex Aeterna
Max Level: 1
Requirement: Lex Divina 5
Skill Form: Active
Type: Debuff
Target: 1 Character
Description: Weakens an enemy so that it
will receive twice as much damage from
the next attack that hits it. Ineffective
against enemies afflicted by the Freezing or
Stone Curse status.
#
PR_MAGNUS#
Magnus Exorcismus
Max Level: 10
Requirement: Turn Undead 3, Lex Aeterna 1
Safety Wall 1
Skill Form: Active
Type: Magical
Target: Ground
Description: Summons a purifying cross on
the ground to inflicts 100% MATK
magical damage on an enemy once per
3 seconds and 130% MATK magical
damage per hit for Devil, Immortal,
Dark and Immortal targets.
Consumes 1 Blue Gemstone.
[Lv 1]: Hits: 1, Duration: 4 seconds
[Lv 2]: Hits: 2, Duration: 5 seconds
[Lv 3]: Hits: 3, Duration: 6 seconds
[Lv 4]: Hits: 4, Duration: 7 seconds
[Lv 5]: Hits: 5, Duration: 8 seconds
[Lv 6]: Hits: 6, Duration: 9 seconds
[Lv 7]: Hits: 7, Duration: 10 seconds
[Lv 8]: Hits: 8, Duration: 11 seconds
[Lv 9]: Hits: 9, Duration: 12 seconds
[Lv 10]: Hits: 10, Duration: 13 seconds
#
WZ_FIREPILLAR#
Fire Pillar
Max Level: 10
Requirement: Fire Wall 1
Skill Form: Active
Type: Magical
Target: Ground
Description: Summons a pillar of flame
on a targeted spot that will deal Fire
elemental magic damage to enemies that come
into contact with it. If no one steps on it
for 30 seconds, it will disappear.
You can place up to 5 Fire Pillars
at once and consumes 1 Blue Gemstone
with level 6 and above. Also it can't be placed
if there is an enemy standing on that cell already
(restriction only applies to War of Emperium areas).
[Lv 1]: Hits: 3/AoE: 3x3 cells
[Lv 2]: Hits: 4/AoE: 3x3 cells
[Lv 3]: Hits: 5/AoE: 3x3 cells
[Lv 4]: Hits: 6/AoE: 3x3 cells
[Lv 5]: Hits: 7/AoE: 3x3 cells
[Lv 6]: Hits: 8/AoE: 7x7 cells
[Lv 7]: Hits: 9/AoE: 7x7 cells
[Lv 8]: Hits: 10 AoE: 7x7 cells
[Lv 9]: Hits: 11/AoE: 7x7 cells
[Lv 10]: Hits: 12/AoE: 7x7 cells
#
WZ_SIGHTRASHER#
Sightrasher
Max Level: 10
Requirement: Sight 1, Lightning Bolt 1
Skill Form: Active
Type: Magical
Target: Enemies around the caster
Description: Attacks enemies by shooting
fireballs, which are first summoned by the
Sight skill, in 8 directions that radiate
away from the caster and inflicting Fire
elemental magic damage as well as
knockback the enemies by 2 cells.
[Lv 1]: MATK 120%
[Lv 2]: MATK 140%
[Lv 3]: MATK 160%
[Lv 4]: MATK 180%
[Lv 5]: MATK 200%
[Lv 6]: MATK 220%
[Lv 7]: MATK 240%
[Lv 8]: MATK 260%
[Lv 9]: MATK 280%
[Lv 10]: MATK 300%

#
WZ_METEOR#
Meteor Storm
Max Level: 10
Requirement: Thunder Storm 1, Sightrasher 2
Skill Form: Active
Type: Magical
Target: Ground
Description: Summons meteors towards a
targeted area to inflict 125% MATK Fire
elemental magic damage upon enemies in an
13x13 area and have a chance to cause Stun.
Each meteorite has a range of 7x7 cells and
depending on skill level, the number of
falling meteorites and the number of hits per
meteorite are increased.
[Lv 1]: Meteorites: 2, Hits: 1, Stun Chance: 3%
[Lv 2]: Meteorites: 3, Hits: 1, Stun Chance: 6%
[Lv 3]: Meteorites: 3, Hits: 2, Stun Chance: 9%
[Lv 4]: Meteorites: 4, Hits: 2, Stun Chance: 12%
[Lv 5]: Meteorites: 4, Hits: 3, Stun Chance: 15%
[Lv 6]: Meteorites: 5, Hits: 3, Stun Chance: 18%
[Lv 7]: Meteorites: 5, Hits: 4, Stun Chance: 21%
[Lv 8]: Meteorites: 6, Hits: 4, Stun Chance: 24%
[Lv 9]: Meteorites: 6, Hits: 5, Stun Chance: 27%
[Lv 10]: Meteorites: 7, Hits: 5, Stun Chance: 30%

Max Level: 10
Requirement: Thunder Storm 1,
Jupitel Thunder 5
Skill Form: Active
Type: Magical
Target: Ground
Description: Collects and drops lightning
strikes on the ground in an 13x13 area around
a designated cell, inflicting Wind elemental
magic damage and probably cause Blind status.
The chance of causing Blind status is reduced
by the immunity of the targets.

[Lv 2]: MATK 600%, Blind Chance: 20%
[Lv 3]: MATK 700%, Blind Chance: 25%
[Lv 4]: MATK 800%, Blind Chance: 30%

[Lv 7]: MATK 1100%, Blind Chance: 45%

[Lv 9]: MATK 1300%, Blind Chance: 55%
[Lv 10]: MATK 1400%, Blind Chance: 60%
#
WZ_WATERBALL#
Water Ball
Max Level: 5
Requirement: Cold Bolt 1, Lightning Bolt 1
Skill Form: Active
Type: Magical
Target: 1 Character
Description: Hurls spheres of water at
high velocity at a targeted enemy.
Caster must be standing in Water in
order to cast Water Ball and cannot be
used in an underwater map. Detection
range of Water cells increases
with skill level and can be used on skill
effects such as Deluge.
[Lv 1]: MATK 130% each Hit, Water cells: 1x1
[Lv 2]: MATK 160% each Hit, Water cells: 3x3
[Lv 3]: MATK 190% each Hit, Water cells: 3x3
[Lv 4]: MATK 220% each Hit, Water cells: 5x5
[Lv 5]: MATK 250% each Hit, Water cells: 5x5
#
WZ_ICEWALL#
Ice Wall
Max Level: 10
Requirement: Stone Curse 1, Frost Diver 1
Skill Form: Active
Type: Supportive
Target: Ground
Description: Creates a frozen wall
of ice in 1x5 cells that will block enemies,
but it can be destroyed using attacks.
The HP of the Ice Wall depends
on skill level and when placed, it's HP is
reduced by 50 per second. It will disappear
when it's HP reaches zero.
[Lv 1]: Ice Wall HP: 400
[Lv 2]: Ice Wall HP: 600
[Lv 3]: Ice Wall HP: 800
[Lv 4]: Ice Wall HP: 1000
[Lv 5]: Ice Wall HP: 1200
[Lv 6]: Ice Wall HP: 1400
[Lv 7]: Ice Wall HP: 1600
[Lv 8]: Ice Wall HP: 1800
[Lv 9]: Ice Wall HP: 2000
[Lv 10]: Ice Wall HP: 2200

Skill Form: Active
Type: Magical
Target: Enemies around the caster

magical damage to enemies within 5x5 cells

Freezing chance and duration increases
with the skill level.

Freeing Chance: 38%

Freeing Chance: 43%
[Lv 3]: Freezing Time: 4.5 seconds,
Freeing Chance: 48%

Freeing Chance: 53%
[Lv 5]: Freezing Time: 7.5 seconds,
Freeing Chance: 58%
[Lv 6]: Freezing Time: 9.0 seconds,
Freeing Chance: 63%
[Lv 7]: Freezing Time: 10.5 seconds,
Freeing Chance: 68%
[Lv 8]: Freezing Time: 12.0 seconds,
Freeing Chance: 73%
[Lv 9]: Freezing Time: 13.5 seconds,
Freeing Chance: 78%
[Lv 10]: Freezing Time: 15.0 seconds,
Freeing Chance: 83%
#
WZ_STORMGUST#
Storm Gust
Max Level: 10
Requirement: Frost Diver 1,
Jupitel Thunder 3
Skill Form: Active
Type: Magical
Target: Ground
Description: Summons a blizzard on the ground
in an 11x11 area around an designated cell
to inflict Water elemental magic damage on
enemies. The blizzard lasts for 4.5 seconds
hitting once every 0.45 seconds and knocking
back enemies by 2 cells. Damaged targets have
a certain chance to be inflicted with
Freezing status and do not take damage while
the Freezing status is active.
[Lv 1]: MATK 120% each Hit
[Lv 2]: MATK 170% each Hit
[Lv 3]: MATK 220% each Hit
[Lv 4]: MATK 270% each Hit
[Lv 5]: MATK 320% each Hit
[Lv 6]: MATK 370% each Hit
[Lv 7]: MATK 420% each Hit
[Lv 8]: MATK 470% each Hit
[Lv 9]: MATK 520% each Hit
[Lv 10]: MATK 570% each Hit

Max Level: 5
9Requirement: Earth Spike 3
4190Requirement: Earth Spike 3
16Requirement: Earth Spike 1
Skill Form: Active
Type: Magical
Target: Ground
Description: Commands the ground in a
targeted area to rise into spikes in
order to deal Earth elemental magic damage
enemies within a 5x5 area. If there is a target
hidden in the ground within that range, it also
deals damage to that target and releases
it from Hiding.
[Lv 1]: MATK 125%
[Lv 2]: MATK 250%
[Lv 3]: MATK 375%
[Lv 4]: MATK 500%
[Lv 5]: MATK 625%
#
WZ_QUAGMIRE#
Quagmire
Max Level: 5
Requirement: Heaven's Drive 1
Skill Form: Active
Type: Debuff
Target: Ground
Description: Temporarily converts
targeted area into marshland that will
reduce the Movement Speed, AGI and DEX
of all targets by half. AGI and DEX
can be reduced maximum by 50. Players
will only receive half of the effect.
[Lv 1]: AGI/DEX -10%, Duration: 5 seconds
[Lv 2]: AGI/DEX -20%, Duration: 10 seconds
[Lv 3]: AGI/DEX -30%, Duration: 15 seconds
[Lv 4]: AGI/DEX -40%, Duration: 20 seconds
[Lv 5]: AGI/DEX -50%, Duration: 25 seconds

with the skill level. DEX and LUK
further increases the success rate.
[Lv 1]: Success Chance: 45%
[Lv 2]: Success Chance: 50%
[Lv 3]: Success Chance: 55%
[Lv 4]: Success Chance: 60%
[Lv 5]: Success Chance: 65%

Requirement: Iron Tempering 1
Skill Form: Passive
Description: Enables the tempering of

[Lv 3]: Success Chance: 45%

[Lv 5]: Success Chance: 55%

BS_ENCHANTEDSTONE#
Enchanted Stone Craft
Max Level: 5
Requirement: Iron Tempering 1
Skill Form: Passive
Description: Enables the refining of
enchanted ores with 10 Green Live, 10
Wind of Verdures, 10 Red Blood, 10
Crystal Blue or 10 Star Dust with a
Mini Furnace. The success rate increases
with the skill level. DEX and LUK
further increases the success rate.
[Lv 1]: Success Chance: 15%
[Lv 2]: Success Chance: 20%
[Lv 3]: Success Chance: 25%
[Lv 4]: Success Chance: 30%
[Lv 5]: Success Chance: 35%

Research Oridecon
Max Level: 5
Requirement: Enchanted Stone Craft 1
Skill Form: Passive
Description: Increases the success rate
of forging items with Oridecon.

[Lv 2]: 7 cell sliding range, Duration: 240 seconds
[Lv 3]: 8 cell sliding range, Duration: 180 seconds
[Lv 4]: 9 cell sliding range, Duration: 120 seconds
[Lv 5]: 10 cell sliding range, Duration: 60 seconds
#
HT_LANDMINE#
Land Mine
Max Level: 5
Skill Form: Active
Type: Trap (Hidden)
Target: Ground
Description: Sets a trap with a 3x3 range
which will explode once an enemy steps on it.
Damage increases according to skill level,
caster's Base Level, DEX and INT.
A trap that has not been stepped on is released
after the duration expires and returns the Trap.
Consumes 1 Trap.
[Lv 1]: Duration: 200 seconds
[Lv 2]: Duration: 160 seconds
[Lv 3]: Duration: 120 seconds
[Lv 4]: Duration: 80 seconds
[Lv 5]: Duration: 40 seconds
#

Requirement: Skid Trap 1
Skill Form: Active
Type: Trap (Hidden)
Target: Ground

which will bind an enemy to the ground for the
skill's duration, which is affected by the AGI

returns the Trap.
Consumes 1 Trap.

[Lv 2]: Duration: 200 seconds
[Lv 3]: Duration: 150 seconds
[Lv 4]: Duration: 100 seconds
[Lv 5]: Duration: 50 seconds

SP and are cast at half of the skill level of

which will be casted and the chance to autocast
depends on your Auto Spells skill level.

lower than the half of Auto Spell, autocast the
learned skill level instead. Spells that are not
learned by the caster can't be casted.

Soul Strike + Fire Ball

designations.
Only 1 monster can be designated for
each cosmic alignment.
[Lv 1]: Designate monster with Sun
alignment. Limited to Small sized
monsters.
[Lv 2]: Designate monster with Moon
alignment. Limited to Medium sized
monsters with MaxHP of 6,000 or more.
[Lv 3]: Designate monster with Star
alignment. Limited to Large sized
monsters with MaxHP of 20,000 or more.
On players, character jobs can be
designated without regarding size and
MaxHP.
#
SG_SUN_ANGER#
Anger of The Sun
Max Level: 3
Requirement: Hatred of Sun Moon Star 1
Skill Form: Passive
Description: Increases Attack Power
against Sun aligned monsters.
Character's Base Level, LUK, DEX and
skill's level affect the damage increase.

SG_FUSION#
Fusion of Sun Moon Star
Requirement: Knowledge of Sun Moon Star 9,
 Spirit State
Skill Form: Active
Type: Supportive
Target: Caster
Description: Awakens the caster's hidden
potential for 10 minutes while in a
spirit status, endowing the caster with
attacks that have 100% HIT rate and will
ignore enemy's Defense, and increased
Movement Speed. However, 2% of caster's
HP will be drained with each attack on
monsters and 8% will be drained with
each attack on other players. In this
status, attacking when the caster's
remaining HP is less that 20% of MaxHP
will instantly kill the caster.

Weapon Refine
Max Level: 10
Requirement: Weaponry Research 10
Skill Form: Active
Type: Crafting
Target: Caster
Description: Refines weapons to increase
their grade. Unlike weapon smithing,
which is affected by DEX or LUK, this
skill's success rate is affected by the
caster's Job Level. Whitesmith have
same rate of success as Item Upgrade
NPCs at Job Level 50, has 5% greater
chance of success than Item Upgrade
NPCs at Job Level 60, and have a 10%
greater chance of success at Job Level
70. If upgrade attempt fails, the
weapon will be destroyed.
Level 1 Weapon require 1 Phracon.
Level 2 Weapon require 1 Emveretarcon.
Level 3-4 Weapon requires 1 Oridecon.
[Lv 1]: +1 Maximum Refine Level
[Lv 2]: +2 Maximum Refine Level
[Lv 3]: +3 Maximum Refine Level
[Lv 4]: +4 Maximum Refine Level
[Lv 5]: +5 Maximum Refine Level

TK_MISSION#
Taekwon Mission
Max Level: 1
Requirement: Kihop 5
Skill Form: Active
Type: Ranking (/taekwon)
Target: Caster
Description: This skill is disabled once
the character changes to an advanced job,
such as Star Gladiator or Soul Linker.
Activating this skill will display the current
monster target for the Taekwon Mission.
Taekwon's will earn 1 Taekwon Mission Point
by defeating 100 target monsters.
Completeting the 100 target monster quota will
begin a new Taekwon Mission with a randomly
chosen target. Mini-Boss and Boss
class monsters will never be chosen as
targets. Taekwon's that achieved
the Top 10 Taekwon Ranking by earning
the most Taekwon Mission Points can
link combos with the Tornado Kick,
Roundhouse, Heel Drop and Counter Kick
skills. Taekwon Rankers at Base Level 90
and above with have tripled MaxHP and
MaxSP and will be able to use all
Taekwon skills.

Requirement: Saturday Night Fever 3,
Sinking Melody 3
Skill Form: Active
Type: Ensemble Skill
Target: Enemies around the caster
Description: The caster and at least one
other performer sing, Song of
Destruction causes to double the damage
inflicted on enemies in range for 10 seconds.
The duration further increases based on the
Lesson skill level.
Consumes 10 Neck Protection Candy.
Requires Musical Instrument or Whip.
[Lv 1]: AoE: 11x11 cells
[Lv 2]: AoE: 11x11 cells
[Lv 3]: AoE: 13x13 cells
[Lv 4]: AoE: 13x13 cells
[Lv 5]: AoE: 15x15 cells
#
WM_SATURDAY_NIGHT_FEVER#
Saturday Night Fever
Max Level: 5
Requirement: Dance with Warg 1
Skill Form: Active
Type: Ensemble Skill
Target: Enemies around the caster
Description: When sung by the caster and
at least one other performer, Saturday Night
Fever has a chance to inflict to all
players in range of the caster to enter a limited
Frenzy state. Frenzied targets will
continuously lose HP and SP as well as
lose HIT and FLEE. Item usage is disabled
and those affected will be forced to sit on the
ground for 3 seconds after the Frenzy
wears off. The chances further
increases based on the Lesson skill level.
Can only be used in PvP/Siege area.
Consumes 5 Neck Protection Candy.
Requires Musical Instrument or Whip.
[Lv 1]: AoE: 7x7 cells, HIT -100, FLEE -50
[Lv 2]: AoE: 9x9 cells, HIT -150, FLEE -80
[Lv 3]: AoE: 11x11 cells, HIT -200, Flee -110
[Lv 4]: AoE: 13x13 cells, HIT -250, Flee -140
[Lv 5]: AoE: 15x15 cells, HIT -300, Flee -170
#

the skill level. The skill is cancelled
when the duration expires or when the
max number of hits is reached.

SO_EL_CONTROL#
Elemental Control
Max Level: 4
Requirement: 4 Elemental Analysis 1
Skill Form: Active
Type: Supportive
Target: Elemental
Description: Command a Elemental in Waiting
mode to switch it's mode to Passive (Buff),
Defensive (Defense) or Offensive (Attack).
To switch the elemental into Waiting mode,
caster just commands same order once more.
When Elementals are in Waiting mode,
they recover little amount HP and SP
continuously.
[Lv 1]: Switch Elemental to Passive mode
[Lv 2]: Switch Elemental to Defensive mode
[Lv 3]: Switch Elemental to Offensive mode
[Lv 4]: Switch Elemental to Waiting mode

SO_EL_ANALYSIS#
4 Elemental Analysis
Max Level: 2
Requirement: Flame Launcher 1,
Frost Weapon 1, Lightning Loader 1,
Seismic Weapon 1
Skill Form: Active
Type: Crafting
Target: Caster
Description: Converts Enchanted Ores to
Pure Gemstones and vice versa within
a convert window, which are used as catalyst
items for summoning Elementals.
[Enchanted Ores]
Flame Heart, Rough Wind,
Mystic Frozen and Great Nature
[Pure Gemstones]
Red Blood, Wind of Verdure,
Crystal Blue and Green Live
[Lv 1]: Convert 1 Enchanted Ore into
Pure Gemstones. (Divided item quantities
are randomly decided.)
[Lv 2]: Combine 10 Pure Gemstones into
1 Enchanted Ore (There is chance to fail,
resulting in nothing.)

Fire Insignia
Max Level: 3
Requirement: Summon Agni 3
Skill Form: Active
Type: Supportive
Target: Ground

ground within 3x3 cells. Recover 1% of
HP per 5 seconds if the target is equipped

element monster. Conversely, decrease 1% of
HP per 5 seconds if the target is equipped
with Earth element armor or it is a Earth
element monster. But all the targets that are
inside of skill range recieve 1.5x more damage
from Water element attacks.

depending on the skill level.

is doubled.

Increases magical damage of Fire elemental
skills by 25%.

Description: Summon a hammer of god to
strike. If there is a target afflicted with
Crimson Marker in the targeted area,
Hammer of God drops on the target and
deals damage to nearby targets.
If there is no target afflicted with
Crimson Marker in the targeted area,
Hammer of God will drop on a random
location within the area.
Damage additionally increases according
to the amount of Coins used and
the caster's Base Level.
Consumes all Coins.
Requires Rifle class weapon.
[Lv 1]: ATK 100%/AoE: 5x5 cells
[Lv 2]: ATK 200%/AoE: 5x5 cells
[Lv 3]: ATK 300%/AoE: 5x5 cells
[Lv 4]: ATK 400%/AoE: 5x5 cells

Skill Form: Active
Type: Special Physical
Target: 1 Character

the air. The enemies lifted can not attack or
move and when Gravity Control is canceled,
they will fall and take Neutral elemental damage.
The falling damage is determined by the
caster's Attack Power and the target's Weight.
If you use it again on the lifted target,
the skill will be canceled.

Requirement: P.F.I 1
Skill Form: Active (Toggle)
Type: Supportive
Target: Caster

Movement is not possible in Intensive Aim
state and an Aiming Count
is accumulated by 1 every 0.5 seconds.
The Aiming Count can be accumulated
up to 10 and if the caster leaves the standing cell
due to certain effects such as being Knockback,
the Intensive Aim state is canceled
and the Aiming Count is reset.
Increases ATK, HIT and Critical while
your are in Intensive Aim state.

NW_THE_VIGILANTE_AT_NIGHT#
The Vigilante at Night
Max Level: 5
Requirement: P.F.I 3, Intensive Aim 1
Skill Form: Active
Type: Physical Ranged
Target: Caster
Recover: 1 AP
Description: Fires around the caster
without mercy, inflicting ranged physical damage
to the surrounding enemies around the caster.
If a Gatling Gun is equipped,
ignores physical defense of targets
and attacks 7 times.
If a Shotgun is equipped, attacks 4 times.

the skill consumes all the Aiming Count
and skill damage is increased according
to the amount of Aiming Count consumed.
Damage is additionaly increased according
on the caster's Base Level and CON.
Consumes 15 Bullets.
Requires a Gatling Gun or Shotgun.
[Lv 1]: Gatling Gun: ATK 300+
(Aiming Count x100)% per Hit/AoE: 11x11 cells
Shotgun: ATK 1500+
(Aiming Count x200)% per Hit/AoE: 5x5 cells

(Aiming Count x400)% per Hit/AoE: 5x5 cells

(Aiming Count x300)% per Hit/AoE: 11x11 cells
Shotgun: ATK 2900+
(Aiming Count x600)% per Hit/AoE: 5x5 cells
[Lv 4]: Gatling Gun: ATK 1200+
(Aiming Count x400)% per Hit/AoE: 13x13 cells
Shotgun: ATK 3600+
(Aiming Count x800)% per Hit/AoE: 7x7 cells

Max Level: 5
Requirement: P.F.I 3, Intensive Aim 1
Skill Form: Active
Type: Physical Ranged
Target: 1 Character
Recover: 1 AP
Description: Fires a single shot to the target,
inflicting ranged physical damage.
If a Pistol is equipped,
ignores physical defense of the target.
If a Rifle is equipped,
inflicts half of the total Critical Damage

the skill consumes all the Aiming Count
and skill damage is increased according
to the amount of Aiming Count consumed.
Damage is additionaly increased according
on the caster's Base Level and CON.

HN_DOUBLEBOWLINGBASH#
Double Bowling Bash
Max Level: 10
Requirement: Self Study Tactics 5
Skill Form: Active
Type: Physical Melee
Target: 1 Character
Recover: 2 AP
Description: Inflicts melee physical damage
to the target and surrounding enemies within
7x7 cells and knocks them back.
The number of attacks increases according
to the number of enemies within range,
and attacks up to 5 times.
Damage is additionaly increased according
to the Self Study Tactics skill level,
the caster's Base Level and POW.
The weapon can't be swapped for
1 second after using this skill.
[Lv 1]: ATK 300+(Tactics Lv x3)% per Hit
[Lv 2]: ATK 500+(Tactics Lv x6)% per Hit
[Lv 3]: ATK 700+(Tactics Lv x9)% per Hit

and the explosion range of the meteorites
from 7x7 to 9x9 cells depending on the skill level.

MER_AUTOBERSERK#
Auto Berserk
Skill Form: Passive
Description: Empowered by rage, character enters
condition that is equivalent to Level 10 Provoke
status when HP is reduced to less than 25% of
Max HP. Provoked status lasts until character
HP is restored to more than 25% of Max HP or
if Provoke effect is nullified.
